Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the phases structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 944. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 159 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on phases structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 944. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 159. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the phases structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 806. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 817 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

For the inductive step, we decompose the phases structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 817. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
